- **주요 수행 작업**:
  - 실행 명령어: `.\gradlew test`
  - 파일 생성: `implementation_plan.md`
  - 파일 생성: `task.md`
  - 파일 수정: `build.gradle`
  - 실행 명령어: `.\gradlew publishToMavenLocal`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 파일 생성: `publish-all.ps1`
  - 실행 명령어: `@"
# ==============================================================================
# Messaging Platform - All Modules Build & Private Publish Automation Script
#
# Execution Order (Dependency Chain):
#   1. messaging-payloader   : Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine
#   2. messaging-dispatcher  : MOM/Direct Dispatcher Engine (Depends on messaging-payloader)
#   3. file-processor        : Target Data Partitioning & Secure Storage
#   4. messaging-backend     : Portal REST API Backend
# ==============================================================================

param(
    [switch]`$SkipTests = `$false,
    [string]`$TargetRepository = "local"
)

`$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
`$ScriptDir = Split-Path -Parent `$MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "   Messaging Platform 4-Module Private Publish Pipeline           " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Base Directory : `$ScriptDir" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Skip Tests     : `$SkipTests" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Repository     : `$TargetRepository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Local .m2 Repo : `$HOME\.m2\repository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host ""

`$Modules = @(
    @{
        Name = "messaging-payloader"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-payloader"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-dispatcher"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-dispatcher"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Multi-channel MOM & Direct Dispatcher Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "file-processor"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "file-processor"
        Artifact = "com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Target File Ingestion & Partitioning Processor"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-backend"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-backend"
        Artifact = "com\ma\web\messaging-portal-backend\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Integrated Messaging Portal Backend REST API"
    }
)

`$Results = @()

foreach (`$Module in `$Modules) {
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan
    Write-Host "Processing [`$(`$Module.Name)] (`$(`$Module.Description))" -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan

    if (-not (Test-Path `$Module.Path)) {
        Write-Error "Directory not found: `$(`$Module.Path)"
        exit 1
    }

    Push-Location `$Module.Path
    try {
        `$GradleCmd = if (`$IsWindows -or `$env:OS -like "*Windows*") { ".\gradlew.bat" } else { "./gradlew" }
        
        `$Tasks = @("clean")
        if (-not `$SkipTests) {
            `$Tasks += "test"
        }
        `$Tasks += "publishToMavenLocal"

        Write-Host "Running: `$GradleCmd `$(`$Tasks -join ' ')" -ForegroundColor Gray
        `$Stopwatch = [System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
        
        & `$GradleCmd `$Tasks
        if (`$LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "Gradle task failed with exit code `$LASTEXITCODE in `$(`$Module.Name)"
        }
        
        `$Stopwatch.Stop()
        `$Duration = [math]::Round(`$Stopwatch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, 2)

        # Verify Artifact in Local M2
        `$ArtifactPath = Join-Path "`$HOME\.m2\repository" `$Module.Artifact
        `$JarCount = (Get-ChildItem -Path `$ArtifactPath -Filter "*.jar" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Measure-Object).Count

        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "SUCCESS"
            Duration = "`$Duration s"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = (`$JarCount -gt 0)
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Published Successfully (`$Duration s)" -ForegroundColor Green
    }
    catch {
        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "FAILED"
            Duration = "-"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = `$false
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Failed: `$_" -ForegroundColor Red
        Pop-Location
        break
    }
    finally {
        Pop-Location
    }
    Write-Host ""
}

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "                      SUMMARY REPORT                              " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
`$Results | Format-Table -AutoSize

`$FailedCount = (`$Results | Where-Object { `$_.Status -ne "SUCCESS" } | Measure-Object).Count
if (`$FailedCount -eq 0) {
    Write-Host "All 4 modules have been successfully tested & published to Maven Local!" -ForegroundColor Green
    exit 0
} else {
    Write-Host "Publish pipeline completed with `$FailedCount error(s)." -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}
"@ | Out-File -FilePath "d:\coding-project\2026-project\publish-all.ps1" -Encoding UTF8
`

  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"`

# ==============================================================================
# Messaging Platform - All Modules Build & Private Publish Automation Script
#
# Execution Order (Dependency Chain):
#   1. messaging-payloader   : Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine
#   2. messaging-dispatcher  : MOM/Direct Dispatcher Engine (Depends on messaging-payloader)
#   3. file-processor        : Target Data Partitioning & Secure Storage
#   4. messaging-backend     : Portal REST API Backend
# ==============================================================================

param(
    [switch]`$SkipTests = `$false,
    [string]`$TargetRepository = "local"
)

`$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
`$ScriptDir = Split-Path -Parent `$MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "   Messaging Platform 4-Module Private Publish Pipeline           " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Base Directory : `$ScriptDir" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Skip Tests     : `$SkipTests" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Repository     : `$TargetRepository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Local .m2 Repo : `$HOME\.m2\repository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host ""

`$Modules = @(
    @{
        Name = "messaging-payloader"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-payloader"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-dispatcher"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-dispatcher"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Multi-channel MOM & Direct Dispatcher Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "file-processor"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "file-processor"
        Artifact = "com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Target File Ingestion & Partitioning Processor"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-backend"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-backend"
        Artifact = "com\ma\web\messaging-portal-backend\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Integrated Messaging Portal Backend REST API"
    }
)

`$Results = @()

foreach (`$Module in `$Modules) {
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan
    Write-Host "Processing [`$(`$Module.Name)] (`$(`$Module.Description))" -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan

    if (-not (Test-Path `$Module.Path)) {
        Write-Error "Directory not found: `$(`$Module.Path)"
        exit 1
    }

    Push-Location `$Module.Path
    try {
        `$GradleCmd = if (`$IsWindows -or `$env:OS -like "*Windows*") { ".\gradlew.bat" } else { "./gradlew" }
        
        `$Tasks = @("clean")
        if (-not `$SkipTests) {
            `$Tasks += "test"
        }
        `$Tasks += "publishToMavenLocal"

        Write-Host "Running: `$GradleCmd `$(`$Tasks -join ' ')" -ForegroundColor Gray
        `$Stopwatch = [System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
        
        & `$GradleCmd `$Tasks
        if (`$LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "Gradle task failed with exit code `$LASTEXITCODE in `$(`$Module.Name)"
        }
        
        `$Stopwatch.Stop()
        `$Duration = [math]::Round(`$Stopwatch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, 2)

        # Verify Artifact in Local M2
        `$ArtifactPath = Join-Path "`$HOME\.m2\repository" `$Module.Artifact
        `$JarCount = (Get-ChildItem -Path `$ArtifactPath -Filter "*.jar" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Measure-Object).Count

        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "SUCCESS"
            Duration = "`$Duration s"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = (`$JarCount -gt 0)
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Published Successfully (`$Duration s)" -ForegroundColor Green
    }
    catch {
        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "FAILED"
            Duration = "-"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = `$false
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Failed: `$_" -ForegroundColor Red
        Pop-Location
        break
    }
    finally {
        Pop-Location
    }
    Write-Host ""
}

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "                      SUMMARY REPORT                              " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
`$Results | Format-Table -AutoSize

`$FailedCount = (`$Results | Where-Object { `$_.Status -ne "SUCCESS" } | Measure-Object).Count
if (`$FailedCount -eq 0) {
    Write-Host "All 4 modules have been successfully tested & published to Maven Local!" -ForegroundColor Green
    exit 0
} else {
    Write-Host "Publish pipeline completed with `$FailedCount error(s)." -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}
"@ | Out-File -FilePath "d:\coding-project\2026-project\publish-all.ps1" -Encoding UTF8
`

- **주요 수행 작업**:
  - 실행 명령어: `.\gradlew test`
  - 파일 생성: `implementation_plan.md`
  - 파일 생성: `task.md`
  - 파일 수정: `build.gradle`
  - 실행 명령어: `.\gradlew publishToMavenLocal`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 파일 생성: `publish-all.ps1`
  - 실행 명령어: `@"
# ==============================================================================
# Messaging Platform - All Modules Build & Private Publish Automation Script
#
# Execution Order (Dependency Chain):
#   1. messaging-payloader   : Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine
#   2. messaging-dispatcher  : MOM/Direct Dispatcher Engine (Depends on messaging-payloader)
#   3. file-processor        : Target Data Partitioning & Secure Storage
#   4. messaging-backend     : Portal REST API Backend
# ==============================================================================

param(
    [switch]`$SkipTests = `$false,
    [string]`$TargetRepository = "local"
)

`$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
`$ScriptDir = Split-Path -Parent `$MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "   Messaging Platform 4-Module Private Publish Pipeline           " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Base Directory : `$ScriptDir" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Skip Tests     : `$SkipTests" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Repository     : `$TargetRepository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Local .m2 Repo : `$HOME\.m2\repository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host ""

`$Modules = @(
    @{
        Name = "messaging-payloader"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-payloader"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-dispatcher"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-dispatcher"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Multi-channel MOM & Direct Dispatcher Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "file-processor"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "file-processor"
        Artifact = "com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Target File Ingestion & Partitioning Processor"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-backend"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-backend"
        Artifact = "com\ma\web\messaging-portal-backend\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Integrated Messaging Portal Backend REST API"
    }
)

`$Results = @()

foreach (`$Module in `$Modules) {
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan
    Write-Host "Processing [`$(`$Module.Name)] (`$(`$Module.Description))" -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan

    if (-not (Test-Path `$Module.Path)) {
        Write-Error "Directory not found: `$(`$Module.Path)"
        exit 1
    }

    Push-Location `$Module.Path
    try {
        `$GradleCmd = if (`$IsWindows -or `$env:OS -like "*Windows*") { ".\gradlew.bat" } else { "./gradlew" }
        
        `$Tasks = @("clean")
        if (-not `$SkipTests) {
            `$Tasks += "test"
        }
        `$Tasks += "publishToMavenLocal"

        Write-Host "Running: `$GradleCmd `$(`$Tasks -join ' ')" -ForegroundColor Gray
        `$Stopwatch = [System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
        
        & `$GradleCmd `$Tasks
        if (`$LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "Gradle task failed with exit code `$LASTEXITCODE in `$(`$Module.Name)"
        }
        
        `$Stopwatch.Stop()
        `$Duration = [math]::Round(`$Stopwatch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, 2)

        # Verify Artifact in Local M2
        `$ArtifactPath = Join-Path "`$HOME\.m2\repository" `$Module.Artifact
        `$JarCount = (Get-ChildItem -Path `$ArtifactPath -Filter "*.jar" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Measure-Object).Count

        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "SUCCESS"
            Duration = "`$Duration s"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = (`$JarCount -gt 0)
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Published Successfully (`$Duration s)" -ForegroundColor Green
    }
    catch {
        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "FAILED"
            Duration = "-"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = `$false
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Failed: `$_" -ForegroundColor Red
        Pop-Location
        break
    }
    finally {
        Pop-Location
    }
    Write-Host ""
}

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "                      SUMMARY REPORT                              " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
`$Results | Format-Table -AutoSize

`$FailedCount = (`$Results | Where-Object { `$_.Status -ne "SUCCESS" } | Measure-Object).Count
if (`$FailedCount -eq 0) {
    Write-Host "All 4 modules have been successfully tested & published to Maven Local!" -ForegroundColor Green
    exit 0
} else {
    Write-Host "Publish pipeline completed with `$FailedCount error(s)." -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}
"@ | Out-File -FilePath "d:\coding-project\2026-project\publish-all.ps1" -Encoding UTF8
`

  - 실행 명령어: `.\gradlew test`
  - 파일 생성: `implementation_plan.md`

  - 파일 수정: `build.gradle`
  - 실행 명령어: `.\gradlew publishToMavenLocal`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 파일 생성: `publish-all.ps1`
  - 실행 명령어: `@"
# ==============================================================================
# Messaging Platform - All Modules Build & Private Publish Automation Script
#
# Execution Order (Dependency Chain):
#   1. messaging-payloader   : Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine
#   2. messaging-dispatcher  : MOM/Direct Dispatcher Engine (Depends on messaging-payloader)
#   3. file-processor        : Target Data Partitioning & Secure Storage
#   4. messaging-backend     : Portal REST API Backend
# ==============================================================================

param(
    [switch]`$SkipTests = `$false,
    [string]`$TargetRepository = "local"
)

`$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
`$ScriptDir = Split-Path -Parent `$MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "   Messaging Platform 4-Module Private Publish Pipeline           " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Base Directory : `$ScriptDir" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Skip Tests     : `$SkipTests" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Repository     : `$TargetRepository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Local .m2 Repo : `$HOME\.m2\repository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host ""

`$Modules = @(
    @{
        Name = "messaging-payloader"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-payloader"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-dispatcher"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-dispatcher"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Multi-channel MOM & Direct Dispatcher Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "file-processor"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "file-processor"
        Artifact = "com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Target File Ingestion & Partitioning Processor"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-backend"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-backend"
        Artifact = "com\ma\web\messaging-portal-backend\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Integrated Messaging Portal Backend REST API"
    }
)

`$Results = @()

foreach (`$Module in `$Modules) {
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan
    Write-Host "Processing [`$(`$Module.Name)] (`$(`$Module.Description))" -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan

    if (-not (Test-Path `$Module.Path)) {
        Write-Error "Directory not found: `$(`$Module.Path)"
        exit 1
    }

    Push-Location `$Module.Path
    try {
        `$GradleCmd = if (`$IsWindows -or `$env:OS -like "*Windows*") { ".\gradlew.bat" } else { "./gradlew" }
        
        `$Tasks = @("clean")
        if (-not `$SkipTests) {
            `$Tasks += "test"
        }
        `$Tasks += "publishToMavenLocal"

        Write-Host "Running: `$GradleCmd `$(`$Tasks -join ' ')" -ForegroundColor Gray
        `$Stopwatch = [System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
        
        & `$GradleCmd `$Tasks
        if (`$LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "Gradle task failed with exit code `$LASTEXITCODE in `$(`$Module.Name)"
        }
        
        `$Stopwatch.Stop()
        `$Duration = [math]::Round(`$Stopwatch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, 2)

        # Verify Artifact in Local M2
        `$ArtifactPath = Join-Path "`$HOME\.m2\repository" `$Module.Artifact
        `$JarCount = (Get-ChildItem -Path `$ArtifactPath -Filter "*.jar" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Measure-Object).Count

        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "SUCCESS"
            Duration = "`$Duration s"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = (`$JarCount -gt 0)
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Published Successfully (`$Duration s)" -ForegroundColor Green
    }
    catch {
        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "FAILED"
            Duration = "-"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = `$false
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Failed: `$_" -ForegroundColor Red
        Pop-Location
        break
    }
    finally {
        Pop-Location
    }
    Write-Host ""
}

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "                      SUMMARY REPORT                              " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
`$Results | Format-Table -AutoSize

`$FailedCount = (`$Results | Where-Object { `$_.Status -ne "SUCCESS" } | Measure-Object).Count
if (`$FailedCount -eq 0) {
    Write-Host "All 4 modules have been successfully tested & published to Maven Local!" -ForegroundColor Green
    exit 0
} else {
    Write-Host "Publish pipeline completed with `$FailedCount error(s)." -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}
"@ | Out-File -FilePath "d:\coding-project\2026-project\publish-all.ps1" -Encoding UTF8
`

  - 파일 수정: `build.gradle`

  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"`

# ==============================================================================
# Messaging Platform - All Modules Build & Private Publish Automation Script
#
# Execution Order (Dependency Chain):
#   1. messaging-payloader   : Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine
#   2. messaging-dispatcher  : MOM/Direct Dispatcher Engine (Depends on messaging-payloader)
#   3. file-processor        : Target Data Partitioning & Secure Storage
#   4. messaging-backend     : Portal REST API Backend
# ==============================================================================

param(
    [switch]`$SkipTests = `$false,
    [string]`$TargetRepository = "local"
)

`$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
`$ScriptDir = Split-Path -Parent `$MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "   Messaging Platform 4-Module Private Publish Pipeline           " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Base Directory : `$ScriptDir" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Skip Tests     : `$SkipTests" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Repository     : `$TargetRepository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Local .m2 Repo : `$HOME\.m2\repository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host ""

`$Modules = @(
    @{
        Name = "messaging-payloader"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-payloader"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-dispatcher"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-dispatcher"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Multi-channel MOM & Direct Dispatcher Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "file-processor"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "file-processor"
        Artifact = "com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Target File Ingestion & Partitioning Processor"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-backend"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-backend"
        Artifact = "com\ma\web\messaging-portal-backend\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Integrated Messaging Portal Backend REST API"
    }
)

`$Results = @()

foreach (`$Module in `$Modules) {
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan
    Write-Host "Processing [`$(`$Module.Name)] (`$(`$Module.Description))" -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan

    if (-not (Test-Path `$Module.Path)) {
        Write-Error "Directory not found: `$(`$Module.Path)"
        exit 1
    }

    Push-Location `$Module.Path
    try {
        `$GradleCmd = if (`$IsWindows -or `$env:OS -like "*Windows*") { ".\gradlew.bat" } else { "./gradlew" }
        
        `$Tasks = @("clean")
        if (-not `$SkipTests) {
            `$Tasks += "test"
        }
        `$Tasks += "publishToMavenLocal"

        Write-Host "Running: `$GradleCmd `$(`$Tasks -join ' ')" -ForegroundColor Gray
        `$Stopwatch = [System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
        
        & `$GradleCmd `$Tasks
        if (`$LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "Gradle task failed with exit code `$LASTEXITCODE in `$(`$Module.Name)"
        }
        
        `$Stopwatch.Stop()
        `$Duration = [math]::Round(`$Stopwatch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, 2)

        # Verify Artifact in Local M2
        `$ArtifactPath = Join-Path "`$HOME\.m2\repository" `$Module.Artifact
        `$JarCount = (Get-ChildItem -Path `$ArtifactPath -Filter "*.jar" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Measure-Object).Count

        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "SUCCESS"
            Duration = "`$Duration s"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = (`$JarCount -gt 0)
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Published Successfully (`$Duration s)" -ForegroundColor Green
    }
    catch {
        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "FAILED"
            Duration = "-"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = `$false
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Failed: `$_" -ForegroundColor Red
        Pop-Location
        break
    }
    finally {
        Pop-Location
    }
    Write-Host ""
}

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "                      SUMMARY REPORT                              " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
`$Results | Format-Table -AutoSize

`$FailedCount = (`$Results | Where-Object { `$_.Status -ne "SUCCESS" } | Measure-Object).Count
if (`$FailedCount -eq 0) {
    Write-Host "All 4 modules have been successfully tested & published to Maven Local!" -ForegroundColor Green
    exit 0
} else {
    Write-Host "Publish pipeline completed with `$FailedCount error(s)." -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}
"@ | Out-File -FilePath "d:\coding-project\2026-project\publish-all.ps1" -Encoding UTF8
`

- **주요 수행 작업**:
  - 실행 명령어: `.\gradlew test`
  - 파일 생성: `implementation_plan.md`
  - 파일 생성: `task.md`
  - 파일 수정: `build.gradle`
  - 실행 명령어: `.\gradlew publishToMavenLocal`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 파일 생성: `publish-all.ps1`
  - 실행 명령어: `@"
# ==============================================================================
# Messaging Platform - All Modules Build & Private Publish Automation Script
#
# Execution Order (Dependency Chain):
#   1. messaging-payloader   : Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine
#   2. messaging-dispatcher  : MOM/Direct Dispatcher Engine (Depends on messaging-payloader)
#   3. file-processor        : Target Data Partitioning & Secure Storage
#   4. messaging-backend     : Portal REST API Backend
# ==============================================================================

param(
    [switch]`$SkipTests = `$false,
    [string]`$TargetRepository = "local"
)

`$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
`$ScriptDir = Split-Path -Parent `$MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "   Messaging Platform 4-Module Private Publish Pipeline           " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Base Directory : `$ScriptDir" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Skip Tests     : `$SkipTests" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Repository     : `$TargetRepository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Local .m2 Repo : `$HOME\.m2\repository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host ""

`$Modules = @(
    @{
        Name = "messaging-payloader"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-payloader"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-dispatcher"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-dispatcher"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Multi-channel MOM & Direct Dispatcher Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "file-processor"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "file-processor"
        Artifact = "com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Target File Ingestion & Partitioning Processor"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-backend"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-backend"
        Artifact = "com\ma\web\messaging-portal-backend\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Integrated Messaging Portal Backend REST API"
    }
)

`$Results = @()

foreach (`$Module in `$Modules) {
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan
    Write-Host "Processing [`$(`$Module.Name)] (`$(`$Module.Description))" -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan

    if (-not (Test-Path `$Module.Path)) {
        Write-Error "Directory not found: `$(`$Module.Path)"
        exit 1
    }

    Push-Location `$Module.Path
    try {
        `$GradleCmd = if (`$IsWindows -or `$env:OS -like "*Windows*") { ".\gradlew.bat" } else { "./gradlew" }
        
        `$Tasks = @("clean")
        if (-not `$SkipTests) {
            `$Tasks += "test"
        }
        `$Tasks += "publishToMavenLocal"

        Write-Host "Running: `$GradleCmd `$(`$Tasks -join ' ')" -ForegroundColor Gray
        `$Stopwatch = [System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
        
        & `$GradleCmd `$Tasks
        if (`$LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "Gradle task failed with exit code `$LASTEXITCODE in `$(`$Module.Name)"
        }
        
        `$Stopwatch.Stop()
        `$Duration = [math]::Round(`$Stopwatch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, 2)

        # Verify Artifact in Local M2
        `$ArtifactPath = Join-Path "`$HOME\.m2\repository" `$Module.Artifact
        `$JarCount = (Get-ChildItem -Path `$ArtifactPath -Filter "*.jar" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Measure-Object).Count

        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "SUCCESS"
            Duration = "`$Duration s"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = (`$JarCount -gt 0)
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Published Successfully (`$Duration s)" -ForegroundColor Green
    }
    catch {
        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "FAILED"
            Duration = "-"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = `$false
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Failed: `$_" -ForegroundColor Red
        Pop-Location
        break
    }
    finally {
        Pop-Location
    }
    Write-Host ""
}

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "                      SUMMARY REPORT                              " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
`$Results | Format-Table -AutoSize

`$FailedCount = (`$Results | Where-Object { `$_.Status -ne "SUCCESS" } | Measure-Object).Count
if (`$FailedCount -eq 0) {
    Write-Host "All 4 modules have been successfully tested & published to Maven Local!" -ForegroundColor Green
    exit 0
} else {
    Write-Host "Publish pipeline completed with `$FailedCount error(s)." -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}
"@ | Out-File -FilePath "d:\coding-project\2026-project\publish-all.ps1" -Encoding UTF8
`

# ==============================================================================
# Messaging Platform - All Modules Build & Private Publish Automation Script
#
# Execution Order (Dependency Chain):
#   1. messaging-payloader   : Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine
#   2. messaging-dispatcher  : MOM/Direct Dispatcher Engine (Depends on messaging-payloader)
#   3. file-processor        : Target Data Partitioning & Secure Storage
#   4. messaging-backend     : Portal REST API Backend
# ==============================================================================

param(
    [switch]`$SkipTests = `$false,
    [string]`$TargetRepository = "local"
)

`$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
`$ScriptDir = Split-Path -Parent `$MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "   Messaging Platform 4-Module Private Publish Pipeline           " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Base Directory : `$ScriptDir" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Skip Tests     : `$SkipTests" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Repository     : `$TargetRepository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Local .m2 Repo : `$HOME\.m2\repository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host ""

`$Modules = @(
    @{
        Name = "messaging-payloader"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-payloader"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-dispatcher"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-dispatcher"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Multi-channel MOM & Direct Dispatcher Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "file-processor"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "file-processor"
        Artifact = "com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Target File Ingestion & Partitioning Processor"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-backend"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-backend"
        Artifact = "com\ma\web\messaging-portal-backend\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Integrated Messaging Portal Backend REST API"
    }
)

`$Results = @()

foreach (`$Module in `$Modules) {
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan
    Write-Host "Processing [`$(`$Module.Name)] (`$(`$Module.Description))" -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan

    if (-not (Test-Path `$Module.Path)) {
        Write-Error "Directory not found: `$(`$Module.Path)"
        exit 1
    }

    Push-Location `$Module.Path
    try {
        `$GradleCmd = if (`$IsWindows -or `$env:OS -like "*Windows*") { ".\gradlew.bat" } else { "./gradlew" }
        
        `$Tasks = @("clean")
        if (-not `$SkipTests) {
            `$Tasks += "test"
        }
        `$Tasks += "publishToMavenLocal"

        Write-Host "Running: `$GradleCmd `$(`$Tasks -join ' ')" -ForegroundColor Gray
        `$Stopwatch = [System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
        
        & `$GradleCmd `$Tasks
        if (`$LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "Gradle task failed with exit code `$LASTEXITCODE in `$(`$Module.Name)"
        }
        
        `$Stopwatch.Stop()
        `$Duration = [math]::Round(`$Stopwatch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, 2)

        # Verify Artifact in Local M2
        `$ArtifactPath = Join-Path "`$HOME\.m2\repository" `$Module.Artifact
        `$JarCount = (Get-ChildItem -Path `$ArtifactPath -Filter "*.jar" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Measure-Object).Count

        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "SUCCESS"
            Duration = "`$Duration s"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = (`$JarCount -gt 0)
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Published Successfully (`$Duration s)" -ForegroundColor Green
    }
    catch {
        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "FAILED"
            Duration = "-"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = `$false
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Failed: `$_" -ForegroundColor Red
        Pop-Location
        break
    }
    finally {
        Pop-Location
    }
    Write-Host ""
}

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "                      SUMMARY REPORT                              " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
`$Results | Format-Table -AutoSize

`$FailedCount = (`$Results | Where-Object { `$_.Status -ne "SUCCESS" } | Measure-Object).Count
if (`$FailedCount -eq 0) {
    Write-Host "All 4 modules have been successfully tested & published to Maven Local!" -ForegroundColor Green
    exit 0
} else {
    Write-Host "Publish pipeline completed with `$FailedCount error(s)." -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}
"@ | Out-File -FilePath "d:\coding-project\2026-project\publish-all.ps1" -Encoding UTF8
`

- **주요 수행 작업**:
  - 실행 명령어: `.\gradlew test`
  - 파일 생성: `implementation_plan.md`
  - 파일 생성: `task.md`
  - 파일 수정: `build.gradle`
  - 실행 명령어: `.\gradlew publishToMavenLocal`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 파일 생성: `publish-all.ps1`
  - 실행 명령어: `@"
# ==============================================================================
# Messaging Platform - All Modules Build & Private Publish Automation Script
#
# Execution Order (Dependency Chain):
#   1. messaging-payloader   : Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine
#   2. messaging-dispatcher  : MOM/Direct Dispatcher Engine (Depends on messaging-payloader)
#   3. file-processor        : Target Data Partitioning & Secure Storage
#   4. messaging-backend     : Portal REST API Backend
# ==============================================================================

param(
    [switch]`$SkipTests = `$false,
    [string]`$TargetRepository = "local"
)

`$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
`$ScriptDir = Split-Path -Parent `$MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "   Messaging Platform 4-Module Private Publish Pipeline           " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Base Directory : `$ScriptDir" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Skip Tests     : `$SkipTests" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Repository     : `$TargetRepository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Local .m2 Repo : `$HOME\.m2\repository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host ""

`$Modules = @(
    @{
        Name = "messaging-payloader"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-payloader"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-dispatcher"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-dispatcher"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Multi-channel MOM & Direct Dispatcher Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "file-processor"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "file-processor"
        Artifact = "com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Target File Ingestion & Partitioning Processor"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-backend"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-backend"
        Artifact = "com\ma\web\messaging-portal-backend\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Integrated Messaging Portal Backend REST API"
    }
)

`$Results = @()

foreach (`$Module in `$Modules) {
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan
    Write-Host "Processing [`$(`$Module.Name)] (`$(`$Module.Description))" -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan

    if (-not (Test-Path `$Module.Path)) {
        Write-Error "Directory not found: `$(`$Module.Path)"
        exit 1
    }

    Push-Location `$Module.Path
    try {
        `$GradleCmd = if (`$IsWindows -or `$env:OS -like "*Windows*") { ".\gradlew.bat" } else { "./gradlew" }
        
        `$Tasks = @("clean")
        if (-not `$SkipTests) {
            `$Tasks += "test"
        }
        `$Tasks += "publishToMavenLocal"

        Write-Host "Running: `$GradleCmd `$(`$Tasks -join ' ')" -ForegroundColor Gray
        `$Stopwatch = [System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
        
        & `$GradleCmd `$Tasks
        if (`$LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "Gradle task failed with exit code `$LASTEXITCODE in `$(`$Module.Name)"
        }
        
        `$Stopwatch.Stop()
        `$Duration = [math]::Round(`$Stopwatch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, 2)

        # Verify Artifact in Local M2
        `$ArtifactPath = Join-Path "`$HOME\.m2\repository" `$Module.Artifact
        `$JarCount = (Get-ChildItem -Path `$ArtifactPath -Filter "*.jar" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Measure-Object).Count

        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "SUCCESS"
            Duration = "`$Duration s"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = (`$JarCount -gt 0)
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Published Successfully (`$Duration s)" -ForegroundColor Green
    }
    catch {
        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "FAILED"
            Duration = "-"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = `$false
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Failed: `$_" -ForegroundColor Red
        Pop-Location
        break
    }
    finally {
        Pop-Location
    }
    Write-Host ""
}

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "                      SUMMARY REPORT                              " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
`$Results | Format-Table -AutoSize

`$FailedCount = (`$Results | Where-Object { `$_.Status -ne "SUCCESS" } | Measure-Object).Count
if (`$FailedCount -eq 0) {
    Write-Host "All 4 modules have been successfully tested & published to Maven Local!" -ForegroundColor Green
    exit 0
} else {
    Write-Host "Publish pipeline completed with `$FailedCount error(s)." -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}
"@ | Out-File -FilePath "d:\coding-project\2026-project\publish-all.ps1" -Encoding UTF8
`

# ==============================================================================
# Messaging Platform - All Modules Build & Private Publish Automation Script
#
# Execution Order (Dependency Chain):
#   1. messaging-payloader   : Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine
#   2. messaging-dispatcher  : MOM/Direct Dispatcher Engine (Depends on messaging-payloader)
#   3. file-processor        : Target Data Partitioning & Secure Storage
#   4. messaging-backend     : Portal REST API Backend
# ==============================================================================

param(
    [switch]`$SkipTests = `$false,
    [string]`$TargetRepository = "local"
)

`$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
`$ScriptDir = Split-Path -Parent `$MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "   Messaging Platform 4-Module Private Publish Pipeline           " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Base Directory : `$ScriptDir" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Skip Tests     : `$SkipTests" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Repository     : `$TargetRepository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Local .m2 Repo : `$HOME\.m2\repository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host ""

`$Modules = @(
    @{
        Name = "messaging-payloader"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-payloader"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-dispatcher"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-dispatcher"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Multi-channel MOM & Direct Dispatcher Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "file-processor"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "file-processor"
        Artifact = "com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Target File Ingestion & Partitioning Processor"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-backend"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-backend"
        Artifact = "com\ma\web\messaging-portal-backend\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Integrated Messaging Portal Backend REST API"
    }
)

`$Results = @()

foreach (`$Module in `$Modules) {
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan
    Write-Host "Processing [`$(`$Module.Name)] (`$(`$Module.Description))" -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan

    if (-not (Test-Path `$Module.Path)) {
        Write-Error "Directory not found: `$(`$Module.Path)"
        exit 1
    }

    Push-Location `$Module.Path
    try {
        `$GradleCmd = if (`$IsWindows -or `$env:OS -like "*Windows*") { ".\gradlew.bat" } else { "./gradlew" }
        
        `$Tasks = @("clean")
        if (-not `$SkipTests) {
            `$Tasks += "test"
        }
        `$Tasks += "publishToMavenLocal"

        Write-Host "Running: `$GradleCmd `$(`$Tasks -join ' ')" -ForegroundColor Gray
        `$Stopwatch = [System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
        
        & `$GradleCmd `$Tasks
        if (`$LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "Gradle task failed with exit code `$LASTEXITCODE in `$(`$Module.Name)"
        }
        
        `$Stopwatch.Stop()
        `$Duration = [math]::Round(`$Stopwatch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, 2)

        # Verify Artifact in Local M2
        `$ArtifactPath = Join-Path "`$HOME\.m2\repository" `$Module.Artifact
        `$JarCount = (Get-ChildItem -Path `$ArtifactPath -Filter "*.jar" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Measure-Object).Count

        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "SUCCESS"
            Duration = "`$Duration s"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = (`$JarCount -gt 0)
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Published Successfully (`$Duration s)" -ForegroundColor Green
    }
    catch {
        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "FAILED"
            Duration = "-"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = `$false
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Failed: `$_" -ForegroundColor Red
        Pop-Location
        break
    }
    finally {
        Pop-Location
    }
    Write-Host ""
}

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "                      SUMMARY REPORT                              " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
`$Results | Format-Table -AutoSize

`$FailedCount = (`$Results | Where-Object { `$_.Status -ne "SUCCESS" } | Measure-Object).Count
if (`$FailedCount -eq 0) {
    Write-Host "All 4 modules have been successfully tested & published to Maven Local!" -ForegroundColor Green
    exit 0
} else {
    Write-Host "Publish pipeline completed with `$FailedCount error(s)." -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}
"@ | Out-File -FilePath "d:\coding-project\2026-project\publish-all.ps1" -Encoding UTF8
`

  - 실행 명령어: `.\gradlew publishToMavenLocal`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 파일 생성: `publish-all.ps1`
  - 실행 명령어: `@"
# ==============================================================================
# Messaging Platform - All Modules Build & Private Publish Automation Script
#
# Execution Order (Dependency Chain):
#   1. messaging-payloader   : Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine
#   2. messaging-dispatcher  : MOM/Direct Dispatcher Engine (Depends on messaging-payloader)
#   3. file-processor        : Target Data Partitioning & Secure Storage
#   4. messaging-backend     : Portal REST API Backend
# ==============================================================================

param(
    [switch]`$SkipTests = `$false,
    [string]`$TargetRepository = "local"
)

`$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
`$ScriptDir = Split-Path -Parent `$MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "   Messaging Platform 4-Module Private Publish Pipeline           " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Base Directory : `$ScriptDir" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Skip Tests     : `$SkipTests" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Repository     : `$TargetRepository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Local .m2 Repo : `$HOME\.m2\repository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host ""

`$Modules = @(
    @{
        Name = "messaging-payloader"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-payloader"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-dispatcher"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-dispatcher"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Multi-channel MOM & Direct Dispatcher Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "file-processor"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "file-processor"
        Artifact = "com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Target File Ingestion & Partitioning Processor"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-backend"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-backend"
        Artifact = "com\ma\web\messaging-portal-backend\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Integrated Messaging Portal Backend REST API"
    }
)

`$Results = @()

foreach (`$Module in `$Modules) {
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan
    Write-Host "Processing [`$(`$Module.Name)] (`$(`$Module.Description))" -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan

    if (-not (Test-Path `$Module.Path)) {
        Write-Error "Directory not found: `$(`$Module.Path)"
        exit 1
    }

    Push-Location `$Module.Path
    try {
        `$GradleCmd = if (`$IsWindows -or `$env:OS -like "*Windows*") { ".\gradlew.bat" } else { "./gradlew" }
        
        `$Tasks = @("clean")
        if (-not `$SkipTests) {
            `$Tasks += "test"
        }
        `$Tasks += "publishToMavenLocal"

        Write-Host "Running: `$GradleCmd `$(`$Tasks -join ' ')" -ForegroundColor Gray
        `$Stopwatch = [System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
        
        & `$GradleCmd `$Tasks
        if (`$LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "Gradle task failed with exit code `$LASTEXITCODE in `$(`$Module.Name)"
        }
        
        `$Stopwatch.Stop()
        `$Duration = [math]::Round(`$Stopwatch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, 2)

        # Verify Artifact in Local M2
        `$ArtifactPath = Join-Path "`$HOME\.m2\repository" `$Module.Artifact
        `$JarCount = (Get-ChildItem -Path `$ArtifactPath -Filter "*.jar" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Measure-Object).Count

        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "SUCCESS"
            Duration = "`$Duration s"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = (`$JarCount -gt 0)
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Published Successfully (`$Duration s)" -ForegroundColor Green
    }
    catch {
        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "FAILED"
            Duration = "-"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = `$false
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Failed: `$_" -ForegroundColor Red
        Pop-Location
        break
    }
    finally {
        Pop-Location
    }
    Write-Host ""
}

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "                      SUMMARY REPORT                              " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
`$Results | Format-Table -AutoSize

`$FailedCount = (`$Results | Where-Object { `$_.Status -ne "SUCCESS" } | Measure-Object).Count
if (`$FailedCount -eq 0) {
    Write-Host "All 4 modules have been successfully tested & published to Maven Local!" -ForegroundColor Green
    exit 0
} else {
    Write-Host "Publish pipeline completed with `$FailedCount error(s)." -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}
"@ | Out-File -FilePath "d:\coding-project\2026-project\publish-all.ps1" -Encoding UTF8
`

  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 파일 생성: `publish-all.ps1`
  - 실행 명령어: `@"
# ==============================================================================
# Messaging Platform - All Modules Build & Private Publish Automation Script
#
# Execution Order (Dependency Chain):
#   1. messaging-payloader   : Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine
#   2. messaging-dispatcher  : MOM/Direct Dispatcher Engine (Depends on messaging-payloader)
#   3. file-processor        : Target Data Partitioning & Secure Storage
#   4. messaging-backend     : Portal REST API Backend
# ==============================================================================

param(
    [switch]`$SkipTests = `$false,
    [string]`$TargetRepository = "local"
)

`$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
`$ScriptDir = Split-Path -Parent `$MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "   Messaging Platform 4-Module Private Publish Pipeline           " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Base Directory : `$ScriptDir" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Skip Tests     : `$SkipTests" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Repository     : `$TargetRepository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Local .m2 Repo : `$HOME\.m2\repository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host ""

`$Modules = @(
    @{
        Name = "messaging-payloader"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-payloader"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-dispatcher"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-dispatcher"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Multi-channel MOM & Direct Dispatcher Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "file-processor"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "file-processor"
        Artifact = "com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Target File Ingestion & Partitioning Processor"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-backend"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-backend"
        Artifact = "com\ma\web\messaging-portal-backend\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Integrated Messaging Portal Backend REST API"
    }
)

`$Results = @()

foreach (`$Module in `$Modules) {
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan
    Write-Host "Processing [`$(`$Module.Name)] (`$(`$Module.Description))" -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan

    if (-not (Test-Path `$Module.Path)) {
        Write-Error "Directory not found: `$(`$Module.Path)"
        exit 1
    }

    Push-Location `$Module.Path
    try {
        `$GradleCmd = if (`$IsWindows -or `$env:OS -like "*Windows*") { ".\gradlew.bat" } else { "./gradlew" }
        
        `$Tasks = @("clean")
        if (-not `$SkipTests) {
            `$Tasks += "test"
        }
        `$Tasks += "publishToMavenLocal"

        Write-Host "Running: `$GradleCmd `$(`$Tasks -join ' ')" -ForegroundColor Gray
        `$Stopwatch = [System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
        
        & `$GradleCmd `$Tasks
        if (`$LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "Gradle task failed with exit code `$LASTEXITCODE in `$(`$Module.Name)"
        }
        
        `$Stopwatch.Stop()
        `$Duration = [math]::Round(`$Stopwatch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, 2)

        # Verify Artifact in Local M2
        `$ArtifactPath = Join-Path "`$HOME\.m2\repository" `$Module.Artifact
        `$JarCount = (Get-ChildItem -Path `$ArtifactPath -Filter "*.jar" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Measure-Object).Count

        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "SUCCESS"
            Duration = "`$Duration s"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = (`$JarCount -gt 0)
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Published Successfully (`$Duration s)" -ForegroundColor Green
    }
    catch {
        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "FAILED"
            Duration = "-"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = `$false
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Failed: `$_" -ForegroundColor Red
        Pop-Location
        break
    }
    finally {
        Pop-Location
    }
    Write-Host ""
}

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "                      SUMMARY REPORT                              " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
`$Results | Format-Table -AutoSize

`$FailedCount = (`$Results | Where-Object { `$_.Status -ne "SUCCESS" } | Measure-Object).Count
if (`$FailedCount -eq 0) {
    Write-Host "All 4 modules have been successfully tested & published to Maven Local!" -ForegroundColor Green
    exit 0
} else {
    Write-Host "Publish pipeline completed with `$FailedCount error(s)." -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}
"@ | Out-File -FilePath "d:\coding-project\2026-project\publish-all.ps1" -Encoding UTF8
`

# ==============================================================================
# Messaging Platform - All Modules Build & Private Publish Automation Script
#
# Execution Order (Dependency Chain):
#   1. messaging-payloader   : Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine
#   2. messaging-dispatcher  : MOM/Direct Dispatcher Engine (Depends on messaging-payloader)
#   3. file-processor        : Target Data Partitioning & Secure Storage
#   4. messaging-backend     : Portal REST API Backend
# ==============================================================================

param(
    [switch]`$SkipTests = `$false,
    [string]`$TargetRepository = "local"
)

`$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
`$ScriptDir = Split-Path -Parent `$MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "   Messaging Platform 4-Module Private Publish Pipeline           " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Base Directory : `$ScriptDir" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Skip Tests     : `$SkipTests" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Repository     : `$TargetRepository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Local .m2 Repo : `$HOME\.m2\repository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host ""

`$Modules = @(
    @{
        Name = "messaging-payloader"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-payloader"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-dispatcher"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-dispatcher"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Multi-channel MOM & Direct Dispatcher Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "file-processor"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "file-processor"
        Artifact = "com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Target File Ingestion & Partitioning Processor"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-backend"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-backend"
        Artifact = "com\ma\web\messaging-portal-backend\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Integrated Messaging Portal Backend REST API"
    }
)

`$Results = @()

foreach (`$Module in `$Modules) {
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan
    Write-Host "Processing [`$(`$Module.Name)] (`$(`$Module.Description))" -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan

    if (-not (Test-Path `$Module.Path)) {
        Write-Error "Directory not found: `$(`$Module.Path)"
        exit 1
    }

    Push-Location `$Module.Path
    try {
        `$GradleCmd = if (`$IsWindows -or `$env:OS -like "*Windows*") { ".\gradlew.bat" } else { "./gradlew" }
        
        `$Tasks = @("clean")
        if (-not `$SkipTests) {
            `$Tasks += "test"
        }
        `$Tasks += "publishToMavenLocal"

        Write-Host "Running: `$GradleCmd `$(`$Tasks -join ' ')" -ForegroundColor Gray
        `$Stopwatch = [System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
        
        & `$GradleCmd `$Tasks
        if (`$LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "Gradle task failed with exit code `$LASTEXITCODE in `$(`$Module.Name)"
        }
        
        `$Stopwatch.Stop()
        `$Duration = [math]::Round(`$Stopwatch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, 2)

        # Verify Artifact in Local M2
        `$ArtifactPath = Join-Path "`$HOME\.m2\repository" `$Module.Artifact
        `$JarCount = (Get-ChildItem -Path `$ArtifactPath -Filter "*.jar" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Measure-Object).Count

        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "SUCCESS"
            Duration = "`$Duration s"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = (`$JarCount -gt 0)
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Published Successfully (`$Duration s)" -ForegroundColor Green
    }
    catch {
        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "FAILED"
            Duration = "-"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = `$false
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Failed: `$_" -ForegroundColor Red
        Pop-Location
        break
    }
    finally {
        Pop-Location
    }
    Write-Host ""
}

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "                      SUMMARY REPORT                              " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
`$Results | Format-Table -AutoSize

`$FailedCount = (`$Results | Where-Object { `$_.Status -ne "SUCCESS" } | Measure-Object).Count
if (`$FailedCount -eq 0) {
    Write-Host "All 4 modules have been successfully tested & published to Maven Local!" -ForegroundColor Green
    exit 0
} else {
    Write-Host "Publish pipeline completed with `$FailedCount error(s)." -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}

  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 실행 명령어: `Get-ChildItem -Path "$HOME\.m2\repository\com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"`
  - 파일 생성: `publish-all.ps1`
  - 실행 명령어: `@"
# ==============================================================================
# Messaging Platform - All Modules Build & Private Publish Automation Script
#
# Execution Order (Dependency Chain):
#   1. messaging-payloader   : Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine
#   2. messaging-dispatcher  : MOM/Direct Dispatcher Engine (Depends on messaging-payloader)
#   3. file-processor        : Target Data Partitioning & Secure Storage
#   4. messaging-backend     : Portal REST API Backend
# ==============================================================================

param(
    [switch]`$SkipTests = `$false,
    [string]`$TargetRepository = "local"
)

`$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
`$ScriptDir = Split-Path -Parent `$MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "   Messaging Platform 4-Module Private Publish Pipeline           " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Base Directory : `$ScriptDir" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Skip Tests     : `$SkipTests" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Repository     : `$TargetRepository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Local .m2 Repo : `$HOME\.m2\repository" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host ""

`$Modules = @(
    @{
        Name = "messaging-payloader"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-payloader"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-dispatcher"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-dispatcher"
        Artifact = "com\messaging\messaging-dispatcher\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Multi-channel MOM & Direct Dispatcher Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "file-processor"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "file-processor"
        Artifact = "com\fileprocessor\file-processor\0.0.1-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Target File Ingestion & Partitioning Processor"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-backend"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-backend"
        Artifact = "com\ma\web\messaging-portal-backend\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Integrated Messaging Portal Backend REST API"
    }
)

`$Results = @()

foreach (`$Module in `$Modules) {
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan
    Write-Host "Processing [`$(`$Module.Name)] (`$(`$Module.Description))" -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------------" -ForegroundColor DarkCyan

    if (-not (Test-Path `$Module.Path)) {
        Write-Error "Directory not found: `$(`$Module.Path)"
        exit 1
    }

    Push-Location `$Module.Path
    try {
        `$GradleCmd = if (`$IsWindows -or `$env:OS -like "*Windows*") { ".\gradlew.bat" } else { "./gradlew" }
        
        `$Tasks = @("clean")
        if (-not `$SkipTests) {
            `$Tasks += "test"
        }
        `$Tasks += "publishToMavenLocal"

        Write-Host "Running: `$GradleCmd `$(`$Tasks -join ' ')" -ForegroundColor Gray
        `$Stopwatch = [System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
        
        & `$GradleCmd `$Tasks
        if (`$LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "Gradle task failed with exit code `$LASTEXITCODE in `$(`$Module.Name)"
        }
        
        `$Stopwatch.Stop()
        `$Duration = [math]::Round(`$Stopwatch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, 2)

        # Verify Artifact in Local M2
        `$ArtifactPath = Join-Path "`$HOME\.m2\repository" `$Module.Artifact
        `$JarCount = (Get-ChildItem -Path `$ArtifactPath -Filter "*.jar" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Measure-Object).Count

        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "SUCCESS"
            Duration = "`$Duration s"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = (`$JarCount -gt 0)
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Published Successfully (`$Duration s)" -ForegroundColor Green
    }
    catch {
        `$Results += [PSCustomObject]@{
            Module = `$Module.Name
            Status = "FAILED"
            Duration = "-"
            ArtifactPath = `$Module.Artifact
            JarVerified = `$false
        }
        Write-Host "[$(`$Module.Name)] Failed: `$_" -ForegroundColor Red
        Pop-Location
        break
    }
    finally {
        Pop-Location
    }
    Write-Host ""
}

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "                      SUMMARY REPORT                              " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
`$Results | Format-Table -AutoSize

`$FailedCount = (`$Results | Where-Object { `$_.Status -ne "SUCCESS" } | Measure-Object).Count
if (`$FailedCount -eq 0) {
    Write-Host "All 4 modules have been successfully tested & published to Maven Local!" -ForegroundColor Green
    exit 0
} else {
    Write-Host "Publish pipeline completed with `$FailedCount error(s)." -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}
"@ | Out-File -FilePath "d:\coding-project\2026-project\publish-all.ps1" -Encoding UTF8
`

# Messaging Platform - All Modules Build & Private Publish Automation Script
#
# Execution Order (Dependency Chain):
#   1. messaging-payloader   : Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine
#   2. messaging-dispatcher  : MOM/Direct Dispatcher Engine (Depends on messaging-payloader)
#   3. file-processor        : Target Data Partitioning & Secure Storage
#   4. messaging-backend     : Portal REST API Backend
# ==============================================================================

param(
    [switch]`$SkipTests = `$false,
    [string]`$TargetRepository = "local"
)

`$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
`$ScriptDir = Split-Path -Parent `$MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "   Messaging Platform 4-Module Private Publish Pipeline           " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Base Directory : `$ScriptDir" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Skip Tests     : `$SkipTests" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Write-Host "Repository     : `$TargetRepository" -ForegroundColor Yellow

Write-Host "==================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan

`$Modules = @(
    @{
        Name = "messaging-payloader"

        Artifact = "com\messaging\payloader\messaging-payloader\1.0-SNAPSHOT"
        Description = "Core Messaging Envelope & Payload Validation Engine"
    },
    @{
        Name = "messaging-dispatcher"
        Path = Join-Path `$ScriptDir "messaging-dispatcher"
